# GRETIL Sanskrit texts: catalog

All Sanskrit texts of [GRETIL (Göttingen Register of Electronic Texts in Indian Languages)](https://gretil.sub.uni-goettingen.de/gretil.html), by GRETIL's own sections.

**Texts listed:** 781 (347 Buddhist: the sections *Religious Literature / Buddhist* and *Philosophy / Buddhist*). **Converted:** 766.

## Sections

- Veda / Samhita / Rigveda (3)
- Veda / Samhita / Atharvaveda (2)
- Veda / Samhita / Samaveda (1)
- Veda / Samhita / Black Yajurveda (1)
- Veda / Brahmana (5)
- Veda / Aranyaka (1)
